## 🤔 What is Seedance 2.0?

**Seedance 2.0** is a video generation model developed by **ByteDance**. It is the industry's first model supporting **simultaneous quad-modal input** — image, video, audio, and text.

**Key Features:**

* 🎥 **Text-to-Video** — Generate videos from text descriptions
* 🖼️ **Image-to-Video** — Animate static images into dynamic videos
* 📹 **Video-to-Video** — Transform and extend existing videos
* 🎵 **Audio-Driven** — Generate videos driven by audio input
* 📐 **Up to 1080p resolution**, 4–15 seconds duration
* 🔊 **Auto dubbing & scoring** — Automatic voiceover and background music

#### 📝 Prompt

```
VISUALS:

Scene 1: Hybrid 2D anime and photorealistic style. A breathtaking, sunlit photorealistic frozen lake surrounded by snow-covered pine trees. Gliding elegantly across the ice is the 2D animated character @PhoebeChibi . She is wearing tiny ice skates and flawlessly performing a complex, mid-air spinning trick, displaying her wide-eyed, starry expression from the reference sheet.

Scene 2: Medium shot of her photorealistic mother, bundled tightly in a thick winter coat, standing at the edge of the ice. She is smiling proudly, cheering enthusiastically, and clapping her hands.

Scene 3: @PhoebeChibi lands perfectly on one skate, flashing her cute, winking expression towards her mother. She begins to skate backward at high speed to build momentum for another trick, completely unaware of an open, photorealistic circular ice-fishing hole directly in her path.

Scene 4: Wide shot. In a flash of comical cartoon physics, she skates directly over the open water and drops straight down into the freezing hole in a perfectly straight line.

Scene 5: A split second later, she pops straight back up out of the hole like a cork and lands on the solid ice with a heavy thud. She is now completely encased inside a massive, perfectly cubic, translucent cartoon block of ice. Through the ice, her 2D face is frozen in her shocked, wide-mouthed 'O' expression from the reference sheet. Only her large purple eyes dart back and forth frantically inside the solid block as the scene ends.

AUDIO & DIALOGUE:

Elegant, swelling classical waltz music plays loudly, setting a graceful and triumphant tone.

Scene 1 SFX: The sharp, crisp, and satisfying shhhhk-shhhhk of ice skate blades rapidly carving into solid ice.

Mother (voice filled with pride): That's beautiful, sweetie! You're doing amazing!

Scene 3 SFX: A bright, sparkly anime ting! sound effect as she winks.

Scene 4 SFX: The elegant waltz music violently record-scratches to a dead halt. A comical, high-pitched slide-whistle down, followed immediately by a heavy, realistic SPLASH!

Scene 5 SFX: A sharp, magical CRACKLE-FROST sound, followed by a dense, heavy THUD of a solid ice block hitting the ground. The only sound left is a rapid, cartoonish, high-pitched teeth-chattering noise echoing across the silent lake.
```

### Generate a continuous 15-second, 25-shot photorealistic blockbuster…

![English](https://img.shields.io/badge/lang-English-blue)

#### 📝 Prompt

```
Generate a continuous 15-second, 25-shot photorealistic blockbuster spaghetti-western action sequence with massive Hollywood production value. Ultra-real cinematic photography, authentic 1870s American frontier, weathered practical sets, realistic skin, sweat, dust, leather, wood, metal, horses, smoke, fire and physically accurate sunlight. Epic scale, visceral action, rich natural textures, anamorphic cinema lenses, shallow depth of field, strong golden-hour backlight, realistic motion blur, atmospheric dust and high dynamic range. No stylization, no animation, no CGI-looking characters.

CHARACTER LOCK: One lean, rugged male gunslinger, sun-weathered face, short dark beard, intense eyes, dusty black wide-brim hat, worn dark-brown leather duster over a faded shirt, gun belt, boots and engraved steel revolver. Keep his face, wardrobe, weapons and accumulated dust identical throughout.

SHOT 01 — Epic aerial wide: gunslinger sprints across a vast sun-scorched desert, three mounted outlaws chasing behind.

SHOT 02 — Macro: battered boots slam into dirt, dust exploding toward lens.

SHOT 03 — Leone-style extreme close-up: sweat, dust and razor-focused eyes beneath hat brim.

SHOT 04 — Telephoto tracking: armed riders charge through a wall of dust.

SHOT 05 — Macro insert: hand snaps toward revolver.

SHOT 06 — Side tracking: still sprinting, he draws and fires backward.

SHOT 07 — Impact close-up: bullet blasts a pursuing outlaw’s rifle from his hand.

SHOT 08 — Huge wide: gunslinger dives into a towering red-rock canyon.

SHOT 09 — Drone-style overhead: riders thunder into the narrow canyon behind him.

SHOT 10 — Ground-level shot: a rattlesnake strikes beside his boot.

SHOT 11 — Extreme close-up: revolver muzzle erupts with fire and smoke.

SHOT 12 — Slow-motion low angle: gunslinger leaps over the recoiling snake as dust catches golden sunlight.

SHOT 13 — Whip-pan transition: he bursts from the canyon into a sprawling frontier town.

SHOT 14 — Long-lens tracking: sprinting through the main street as terrified townspeople scatter and gunmen emerge onto balconies.

SHOT 15 — Ground insert: bullets tear through barrels and wooden posts inches from his feet.

SHOT 16 — Low-angle action shot: he dives and slides beneath a speeding horse-drawn wagon.

SHOT 17 — Tight tracking beneath wagon: sliding through dirt, he fires two precise shots between spinning wooden wheels.

SHOT 18 — Wide impact shot: two armed bandits crash backward through saloon doors as splintered wood and dust explode.

SHOT 19 — Steadicam pursuit: gunslinger rolls upright without stopping and races toward the railway.

SHOT 20 — Monumental wide: a full-size steam locomotive suddenly roars across his escape route, black smoke filling the sunset sky.

SHOT 21 — Extreme close-up: his eyes calculate the impossible escape as approaching riders appear behind him.

SHOT 22 — Low-angle hero shot: he grabs the saddle of a galloping riderless horse and swings onto it at full speed.

SHOT 23 — High-speed tracking: horse races alongside the moving locomotive; steam, sparks and dust fill the frame as he launches from the saddle toward a freight car.

SHOT 24 — Blockbuster slow-motion hero shot: he catches the train, climbs onto the roof and stands against the burning sunset while pursuing riders skid to a halt behind.

SHOT 25 — Epic final aerial pullback: locomotive races across an enormous desert landscape toward the horizon. The gunslinger stands alone on the roof, battered duster whipping violently in the wind, lowering his smoking revolver as the camera pulls hundreds of feet back to reveal mountains, railway and endless golden frontier.

EDITING: Extremely fast 25-shot montage compressed into exactly 15 seconds, average 0.6 seconds per shot, with several micro-cuts and selected hero shots held slightly longer. Every cut introduces new action, scale, threat or perspective. Seamless match-on-action, whip pans, foreground wipes and directional cuts maintain continuous left-to-right momentum.

CAMERA: ARRI Alexa 35 cinematic look, anamorphic lens character, 24fps, 2.39:1 widescreen. Mix massive aerial establishing shots, 24mm action wides, 50mm tracking shots, 85mm portraits and 135mm extreme Leone-style close-ups. Realistic handheld vibration during impacts, stabilized high-speed tracking, natural lens flare, subtle anamorphic distortion and physically believable focus pulls.

LIGHTING: Late golden-hour desert sun, hard warm backlight through airborne dust, realistic deep shadows, practical fire and muzzle flashes. Lighting direction remains physically consistent as the chase progresses toward sunset.

ACTION PHYSICS: Heavy, grounded and dangerous. Real horse movement, believable sprinting momentum, recoil, debris, dust displacement, wagon suspension, locomotive mass and wind interaction. No superhero physics except one plausible cinematic train jump. Every action clearly causes the next.

SOUND DESIGN: thunderous horse hooves, boots hitting dirt, leather creaks, revolver cracks with canyon echoes, bullet ricochets, splintering timber, screaming train whistle, locomotive pistons, rushing steam and a powerful orchestral spaghetti-western score with electric guitar, whistles and pounding percussion.

Maintain absolute character consistency, wardrobe continuity, weapon placement, accumulating dirt and damage, environmental geography and left-to-right travel. No gore, no duplicated character, no changing face, no modern objects, no fantasy elements, no plastic CGI skin, no artificial game-engine appearance, no excessive slow motion, no impossible camera teleportation.

Final result should feel like a $200-million theatrical action-western compressed into 15 seconds: photorealistic, enormous scale, practical stunt energy, visceral speed, iconic spaghetti-western framing and premium blockbuster cinematography.
```

### Create a 15-second ultra-realistic Pixar-quality animated short film with…

![English](https://img.shields.io/badge/lang-English-blue)

#### 📝 Prompt

```
Create a 15-second ultra-realistic Pixar-quality animated short film with feature-film cinematography, multiple cinematic shots, expressive facial animation, realistic kitten behavior, premium fur simulation, soft warm morning sunlight, volumetric light rays, shallow depth of field, smooth camera movement, rack focus, cinematic lens changes, and seamless editing. The main character is a beautiful, elegant 23-year-old woman with long soft wavy chestnut hair, striking hazel eyes, wearing a cream knitted sweater, working seriously on a silver laptop at a clean wooden desk inside a cozy modern home office. The second character is an adorable 8-week-old fluffy orange tabby kitten wearing a tiny blue collar. The kitten behaves like a real kitten, never like a human.
Shot 1 (0.0–1.2s) — Extreme close-up of the laptop keyboard. Fingers rapidly typing. Cinematic keyboard sounds. Rack focus from moving fingers to blurred laptop screen filled with work documents.
Shot 2 (1.2–2.3s) — Close-up of the elegant woman's focused face from a 45-degree angle. Her eyes scan the laptop while typing continuously. Warm sunlight creates soft highlights across her face. Background beautifully blurred.
Shot 3 (2.3–3.2s) — Without revealing the kitten yet, a tiny fluffy orange paw slowly enters the frame from below and gently taps the woman's cheek. She pauses for half a second, smiles slightly, gently pushes the paw away with one hand without looking away from the laptop, and immediately continues typing.
Shot 4 (3.2–4.4s) — Reveal shot. Medium-wide cinematic angle. The adorable orange tabby kitten is now fully visible sitting beside the laptop, tail flicking playfully while staring at the woman, wanting attention.
Shot 5 (4.4–5.8s) — Close-up of the kitten's face. It tilts its head left, then right, ears twitching slightly. Big glossy kitten eyes. Tiny soft meow. Extremely cute Pixar facial animation.
Shot 6 (5.8–7.2s) — Medium shot. The kitten gently places both front paws onto the keyboard, interrupting the typing. Keys depress naturally. The woman calmly slides the kitten aside with one hand while continuing to work, barely taking her eyes off the screen.
Shot 7 (7.2–9.0s) — Low-angle tracking shot. The kitten walks across the desk, climbs onto the woman's shoulder, leans toward her ear, and lets out two soft playful meows. The woman's shoulders tense slightly, she exhales, briefly closes her eyes in mild annoyance, then keeps typing.
Shot 8 (9.0–10.6s) — Extreme close-up of the kitten. It realizes the subtle attempts aren't working. One whisker twitches. Tiny determined smile appears. Its eyes slowly shift toward the laptop power button.
Shot 9 (10.6–12.8s) — Macro tracking shot following the kitten's tiny paws walking across the keyboard toward the laptop's top corner. Cinematic rack focus between the kitten's paw and the illuminated power button.
Shot 10 (12.8–14.4s) — Extreme macro close-up. The kitten slowly lifts one fluffy paw in the air. Suspense builds. It gently presses the laptop power button with perfect timing.
Shot 11 (14.4–15.0s) — The laptop instantly powers off. The glossy black screen reflects both the shocked woman and the proud kitten standing beside the laptop. The woman freezes with wide eyes and slightly open mouth. The kitten looks satisfied with a tiny smile. Hold this exact reflection frame naturally for the final half-second. Do NOT fade to black. Do NOT cut away. End on this reflection so the next 15-second extension begins seamlessly from this exact frame.
Style: Pixar feature film, cinematic camera language, multi-shot editing, expressive acting, ultra-detailed fur, physically accurate lighting, natural kitten movements, emotional storytelling, premium animation quality, smooth transitions, no text, no subtitles, no logos, no glitches, consistent character design throughout.
```

### 参照画像の女子高生を主人公として使用する。参照画像に写っている本人の顔立ち、目鼻立ち、輪郭、髪型、髪色、肌の質感、体格、制服、年齢感、全体的な雰囲気を高い精度で…

![日本語](https://img.shields.io/badge/lang-日本語-green)

#### 📝 Prompt

```
参照画像の女子高生を主人公として使用する。参照画像に写っている本人の顔立ち、目鼻立ち、輪郭、髪型、髪色、肌の質感、体格、制服、年齢感、全体的な雰囲気を高い精度で維持し、動画全体を通して同一人物に見えるようにする。
15秒、24fps、縦型9:16。フォトリアルな実写映像。高品質な青春映画や自然なSNS動画のような質感。

主人公は、プロのアイドルや熟練したダンサーではなく、「クラスで一番可愛い子が、放課後の誰もいない教室で、少し勇気を出してTikTokダンスを撮影している」
ような、親しみやすく自然な可愛さを持つ女子高生。

場所は放課後の日本の高校の教室。教室内には主人公以外の人物はいない。机、椅子、黒板、窓、カーテンなどが自然に配置されている。窓から夕方の柔らかな光が差し込み、明るく透明感のある青春らしい雰囲気。背景は整理されており、人物が主役として目立つ。

カメラは教室内に固定されたスマートフォンで撮影しているような正面構図。頭から靴まで全身が映る。手足が画面外に切れないようにし、人物を画面中央付近に保つ。カメラの急激な移動、ズーム、カット切り替えは行わない。

主人公は少し恥ずかしがり屋で、撮影されることに照れている。しかし嫌がっているのではなく、緊張しながらもダンスを楽しんでいる。作り込まれたアイドル笑顔ではなく、時々こぼれる自然な照れ笑いが魅力的。

ダンスはTikTokで流行しそうな、可愛らしく覚えやすい振り付け。両手を使った小さなポーズ、左右への軽いステップ、身体を小さく揺らす動き、手を顔の近くへ運ぶ仕草などを組み合わせる。動きは滑らかだが、完璧すぎず、一般の女子高生が練習して踊っているような自然さを残す。

【時間ごとの動き】
0〜2秒：
撮影開始直後。主人公は最初のダンスポーズで立っている。少し緊張した表情でカメラを見るが、すぐに小さく照れ笑いを浮かべる。
音楽のタイミングに合わせて、控えめにダンスを始める。

2〜5秒：
左右に軽くステップを踏みながら、胸元や顔の近くで両手を使った可愛らしい振り付けを行う。動きは軽やかで自然。
カメラを見ながら踊るが、恥ずかしさから一瞬だけ視線を横に逸らす。
その直後、少し照れた笑顔でもう一度カメラを見る。

5〜7秒：
ダンスの流れの中で、片手を自然に髪へ伸ばし、髪を片方の耳にかける。
単なるポーズではなく、踊っている途中に髪が気になって直したような自然な仕草。
髪を耳にかけた直後、少し恥ずかしそうに微笑みながらダンスへ戻る。

7〜10秒：
振り付けが少しだけ大きくなり、両手を左右へ動かしながら小さく身体を回す。
スカートや髪が動きに合わせて自然に揺れる。
楽しさが少しずつ勝ってきて、笑顔が明るくなる。
ただしプロのアイドルのような完璧な表情にはせず、時々口元を緩める自然な照れ笑いを見せる。

10〜13秒：
ダンスの最後のパート。
カメラ目線で可愛らしい手振りと軽いステップを行い、最後に両手を顔の近くに置いた控えめな決めポーズを取る。決めポーズの直後、自分で恥ずかしくなったように笑う。
ダンス撮影は13秒の時点で完全に終了する。

13〜15秒：
ダンスが終わり、緊張が一気にほどける。
主人公は究極に照れた表情になる。
「恥ずかしい、無理」と言いたそうに、笑いながら首を左右に何度も小さく振る。
その場で両足を小刻みにバタバタさせ、落ち着かないように身体を揺らす。
両手を顔の近くに持ってきたり、口元を隠したりしながら、カメラから一瞬目を逸らす。

Create an ultra-realistic 2-minute cinematic short film featuring the same young East Asian woman and man consistently throughout. Maintain identical facial features, hairstyles, body proportions, and outfits in every scene. Premium Korean drama aesthetic, cozy winter atmosphere, soft cinematic lighting, realistic performances, shallow depth of field, and elegant camera movements.

The story begins 15 years earlier. Two childhood friends play joyfully in the snow, building a snowman, throwing snowballs, and laughing together. Before going home, they sit on a wooden park bench where the boy promises, "When the first snow falls, let's meet here again." They seal the promise with a pinky swear.

The film transitions to the present. The young woman returns to her quiet hometown wearing a warm winter coat, scarf, and boots. She walks through familiar streets, passing her old school, bakery, playground, and frozen river as brief childhood memories flash back. She finally reaches the same park bench and quietly waits, wondering if he ever remembered.

As evening approaches, she stands to leave. At that moment, the season's first snow begins to fall. She looks up as snowflakes drift around her. Across the park, the young man slowly appears through the snowfall wearing a dark wool coat and scarf. They recognize each other instantly, smiling through tears.

He softly says, "I told you I'd come when the first snow fell."

They embrace warmly before walking together through the snow-covered streets, sharing hot chocolate, laughing, and revisiting the places where they grew up. The final scene shows them building a small snowman beside the same park bench before walking hand in hand into the softly falling snow.

Natural ambient sound only—gentle wind, footsteps in snow, distant train, children playing, quiet conversations, and falling snow. No music, subtitles, logos, watermarks, or on-screen text. Ultra-realistic Korean drama cinematography, 4K HDR, 16:9.
```

@图1 为角色一致性参考，人物外貌与服装完全按参考图，不进行任何重新设计。
男性：深棕凌乱短发，异色瞳（左眼翠绿、右眼湛蓝），银色钢制板甲与深棕羊毛斗篷。
女性：深红色长卷发，琥珀橙瞳，象牙白一字肩长裙。
承接前作：两人共乘一匹马疾驰于辽阔草原，巨龙并飞身侧。
晴朗正午，明亮通透的湛蓝天空，洁白积云，强烈明媚日光，草原翠绿鲜亮。
ARRI Alexa 65，Cooke S7变形定焦，T1.4，浅景深，青蓝与翠绿明亮调色，
35mm胶片颗粒与halation，god rays，2.39电影质感，16:9。

[0-3秒] 降落·地面震动：镜头贴地仰视，巨龙从空中俯冲降落在他们正前方，
双翼张开制动掀起环形气浪，尘土与草屑爆散冲向镜头，镜头随落地冲击微微震动，
马匹惊起前蹄嘶鸣，两人猛地勒缰停住，逆光下巨龙轮廓庞大压顶。

[3-6秒] 越肩跟随·靠近：她翻身下马，镜头从男性肩后越肩跟拍她的背影，
前景是他握紧缰绳的手（虚焦），她赤足踏过草地一步步走向巨龙，
裙摆与红发在气流中飘动，rack focus从他的手拉至她远去的背影，
巨龙静静低头注视她走来。

[6-9秒] 微距·接触：极特写——她的手指缓缓伸出，触碰巨龙的鼻吻，
鳞片纹理与她指尖的细节清晰可见，巨龙鼻息吹动她的发丝，
镜头随后缓缓拉远并低角度环绕，揭示一人一龙额头几乎相抵的宁静画面，
阳光在两者之间洒落，尘埃如金粉悬浮。

[9-12秒] 升降机上升·登龙：巨龙缓缓低下头颅与前肢，如同邀请，
她回头看向他并伸出手，他翻身下马握住她的手，两人一同攀上龙背，
镜头以升降机（crane）姿态从地面缓缓升起跟随他们登顶，
草原与蓝天在画面中逐渐展开，马匹在下方安静低头吃草。

[12-15秒] 起飞·垂直拉升揭示：巨龙一声长啸，双翼猛然下压——
草浪以他们为中心呈环形炸开，镜头急速后拉并垂直拉升，
巨龙冲天而起，两人紧贴龙背，她的红发狂舞，他从身后环护住她，
镜头继续升高拉远成史诗大远景：巨龙盘旋升入湛蓝天空与洁白云海之间，
翠绿草原与群山铺满画面下方，画面在明亮阳光中缓慢淡出。

运镜：贴地降落震动→越肩跟随rack focus→微距接触环绕→crane升起登龙→后拉垂直拉升。
电影级摄影，从容有力的运镜，专业调色，超真实皮肤、金属、鳞片与毛发质感，16:9。

配音（日语）：
[6-9秒] 女声（极轻柔，对着巨龙低语）：「怖くない……よ。」
[12-15秒] 男声（低沉温柔，在她耳边）：「行こう。」

环境音：龙翼气浪轰鸣、马嘶、草浪风声、龙的低沉呼吸与一声长啸，
弦乐在第6秒转为温柔，第12秒起管弦乐与鼓点恢宏爆发。

动漫，卡通，3D渲染，CGI，塑料皮肤，假发感，双眼同色，盔甲塑料感，两匹马，龙的造型混乱，翅膀畸形，马匹变形，人物穿模，畸形的手，多余的手指，与参考图不符，黄昏，夕阳，阴天，口型不同步，构图混乱，过度饱和，主体模糊

### 以上传人物定妆图作为唯一女主身份与服装参考，生成一段 7秒、9:16竖屏 的真实生日反差短视频。

#### 📝 Prompt

```
以上传人物定妆图作为唯一女主身份与服装参考，生成一段 7秒、9:16竖屏 的真实生日反差短视频。

【风格】

真实手机竖屏短视频质感，夜晚户外公园，固定机位，一镜到底。
前半段保持自然、温柔的生日氛围，后半段突然进入夸张强反差效果。
动作保持自然真实速度，节奏利落清楚，不要慢动作，不要推拉摇移，不要跟拍，不要数字变焦。
【场景】
夜晚户外公园，画面中心是一张长椅，后方有草地、树木和少量路灯。
开场长椅为空，背景简洁，空间关系清楚。
镜头全程保持同一个位置和构图。
后半段火焰出现后，橙黄色火光真实照亮女主、蛋糕、长椅和附近树木，产生自然动态反光。

【女主】

严格参考上传人物图片，保持人物身份、脸部、发型、身材比例、服装和配饰前后一致，不重新设计人物造型。
女主前半段状态自然，晃动香槟时动作利落。
完成向下摔香槟动作后，不再做额外停顿，立即进入香槟喷射和嘴部喷火的爆发状态。
后半段保持稳定夸张的高潮姿势。
【核心道具】
女主左手始终握着同一瓶绿色香槟，右手始终托着同一个点燃蜡烛的生日蛋糕。
香槟瓶口处于打开状态。
瓶口后续只能喷出：

真实香槟酒液

白色香槟泡沫
细密液体飞沫
瓶口不能喷火，不能出现烟花、火星、冷焰火、银色火柱或魔法粒子。
生日蛋糕始终保持水平，蜡烛持续点燃，不倾倒、不掉落、不变形。

【镜头1：空场建立与女主入场】

固定机位拍摄夜晚公园长椅。
开场先短暂保持空长椅画面，让观众看清长椅、草地、树木和路灯。

随后女主从画面右侧进入。她左手握住绿色香槟瓶，右手平稳托着点燃蜡烛的生日蛋糕，沿长椅前方向画面中心移动。
女主自然走到长椅中央，摄影机始终固定，不能跟随她移动。

【镜头2：坐下并晃动香槟】
女主到达长椅中心后自然坐下，身体位于画面中央。
右手继续将生日蛋糕稳定托在胸前附近，左手握住绿色香槟瓶颈。
女主连续上下晃动香槟瓶大约三次：

香槟瓶抬至肩膀附近 → 快速下降至腰部附近 → 再次抬高 → 再次向下甩动。

每次动作都由手臂带动，节奏清楚，像在增加瓶内压力。身体和蛋糕保持稳定。

【镜头3：向下摔香槟后立刻爆发】

完成晃动后，女主左手将香槟瓶再次抬高，随后做出一次明显、利落的向下摔瓶动作。
动作必须清楚表现为：

抬起香槟瓶 → 手臂快速向下挥动 → 香槟瓶被猛地摔向身体左下方 → 女主仍然牢牢握住瓶颈。

香槟瓶不能脱手，不能飞出去，不能砸到地面，不能破碎。
这是一个“向下猛摔式甩动”的动作，用来触发喷射。

重点：就在向下摔香槟动作完成的瞬间，立刻同时触发两个效果，不要额外停顿，不要先张嘴再等一下。

两个效果必须同一瞬间启动：
左手绿色香槟瓶口垂直向上喷出真实香槟酒液、大量白色泡沫和飞沫

女主嘴部同时向画面右侧喷出橙黄色火焰

人物可以在摔瓶完成的同时顺势张嘴并把头转向画面右侧，让喷火动作与香槟喷射同步发生。

【镜头4：持续喷射并保持高潮姿势】

女主保持坐姿，头部朝向画面右侧，嘴部持续喷出橙黄色火焰。
左手牢牢握住香槟瓶，瓶口继续向上喷出真实香槟酒液、白色泡沫和液体飞沫。

香槟喷射必须具有真实液体物理效果：

透明偏浅金色的酒液

大量白色泡沫

液体飞沫四散
部分酒液自然回落

瓶口周围出现湿润液体反光

香槟瓶口绝对不能喷火。
只有女主嘴部向右喷出火焰。

右手生日蛋糕始终稳定可见，蜡烛保持点燃。
人物身体、五官、服装、香槟瓶和蛋糕不能受到特效拉扯或发生变形。
火焰真实照亮女主脸部、服装、蛋糕和长椅；香槟液体反射周围火光，呈现自然暖色高光。

画面形成三个清楚区域：

左侧：香槟酒液和泡沫

中间：女主与蛋糕
右侧：嘴部喷出的橙黄色火焰

【镜头5：黑白生日海报定格】

香槟喷射和嘴部火焰达到最夸张的高潮状态后，直接使用当前画面进行黑白海报式收尾。
整张画面瞬间切换为黑白并完全定格，女主、蛋糕、香槟喷射和嘴部火焰全部停留在同一个高潮姿态。
画面上方出现白色手写体英文：
Happy Birthday
不能让女主重新摆姿势，不能切换镜头角度，不能渐变褪色、闪白或溶解。
必须由彩色动态高潮画面直接硬切为黑白定格海报。

【声音与节奏】
背景音乐保持连续，节奏清楚。
女主入场、晃动香槟和向下摔香槟的动作跟随音乐节拍。

向下摔香槟时加入短促挥动风声和低频节奏冲击音，但不能出现玻璃破碎声。
就在向下摔香槟动作完成的瞬间，立刻叠加：

香槟高压喷射声

酒液和泡沫持续喷出的嘶响声

嘴部火焰爆燃声
短促低频冲击声
香槟声必须像高压酒液和泡沫喷射，不能像烟花燃烧声。
后半段音乐继续托住高潮，不要突然停掉。
【强制约束】

总时长必须是 7秒

全程 9:16竖屏、固定机位、一镜到底

动作顺序必须是：

香槟液体必须有透明感、泡沫、飞沫、重力回落和湿润反光

火焰和香槟喷射必须在同一瞬间启动

女主、蛋糕和香槟瓶全程保持稳定一致

不要新增人物、额外手臂、额外瓶子或第二个蛋糕

女主角形象描述：东亚女性，长头发，连衣裙，红色高跟鞋，右边大腿上绑着枪套，里面有手枪。

时长：10秒，0.5-1秒一招，全程无站桩动态打斗
场景：90年代美国办公室里，昏暗的顶光，陈旧稍微有脱落的墙皮，有些凌乱。夜晚。
画质：8K超清，电影级胶片质感，极致写实，毛孔级细节，复古绿色滤镜
运镜：电影级复合运镜，低角度仰拍、高速跟拍、环绕瞬移、近景快切、动态升格无缝切换
动作剧情
0-3秒瞬袭开场
1.【0.5s】低角度仰拍特写滑步突进，沉肩重拳直轰敌人面门，反派瞬间倒飞。反派人物大量聚集。
2.【1s】高速跟拍拧身横踢，腿风破空横扫侧边敌人，身形腾空顺势借力发力，连环腿踢倒数人。
3.【2s】半空扭身侧蹬，精准踹击敌人胸口，敌人狠狠撞向墙体，严重受伤，腾起一片灰尘，墙体留下痕迹但没有坍塌
 
3-8秒合围破杀
4.【3.5s】主角极其流畅地抓住一个敌人的手臂，完成一个残暴的柔道过肩摔，将敌人重重砸在操作台上，操作台上的玻璃器皿碎裂，台上的电脑和各种办公用品洒落一地。墙上挂的东西也被震落。物理效果要真实可信。
5.【4.5s】近景快切，迅猛提膝顶击敌人腹腔。一掌把敌人打飞出去。
6.【5.5s】侧身极速躲避攻击，反手锁腕擒拿，臂肘连击敌人胸口两下，敌人倒地。
7.【6.5s】她举枪精准射击敌人膝盖，敌人吃痛躬身。她再次射击中敌人头部。敌人受击反馈真实
8.【7.5s】利落转身回旋重肘，重创身后偷袭反派颈部
9.【8.5s】垫步极速冲刺，凌厉掌刃狠劈敌人肩颈，对手直线倒飞出去受伤倒地
10.【9.5s】后撤步瞬间转身，凌厉后踹直击敌人后腰，敌人痛苦倒地惨叫
11.【10秒】她甩头，跨过地上的敌人身体，走向镜镜头，英雄视角，所有反派尽数倒地
 
负面提示词：无站桩发呆、动作卡顿、慢动作，重复招式，无效招式、穿模畸形、模糊画质、劣质特效、现代建筑、多余路人、血腥画面、扭曲肢体、低帧率、CG塑料感，无光鲜崭新服装，无崭新的家具。
剧烈的枪口火焰，极度暴力的物理打击反馈，毫不留情、完美无瑕的CQB近战枪斗术厮杀。电影级杰作。高对比度，视觉冲击力强，光影效果剧烈。照片级真实感，8K 120帧电影画质，受《疾速追杀》启发的粗粝优雅感，浅景深，全程保持流畅的连续运镜。动作连贯流畅，一气呵成，重拳重腿，拳腿到肉。招式凌厉，尘土飞扬。枪斗术也非常专业。远景面部清晰，画面流畅。动量守恒、反作用力、重力9.8m/s²、关节运动、重心转移、惯性、弹性碰撞、地面反力、空气阻力。无台词仅呼吸声，无音乐。骨骼完整、关节比例正常、无扭曲拉伸、无滑步穿模。16:9。
```

### MARC JACOBS — "SUMMER GLOSS"

![English](https://img.shields.io/badge/lang-English-blue)

#### 📝 Prompt

```
MARC JACOBS — "SUMMER GLOSS"
Lipstick Commercial

**ACTIVE REFERENCES**  
**MODEL REFERENCE**  
Exact same woman as the bag campaign. Identical facial identity, hairstyle, body proportions, confident editorial expression, posture, walking style. Natural makeup base with the hero lipstick applied (rich, vibrant pink-rose gloss). She wears a soft, flowing babypink dress (lightweight silk-cotton blend, slightly billowy sleeves, midi length, elegant yet effortless summer style). Maintain identical identity throughout. No drift.

**@Image 1 — MARC JACOBS LIPSTICK**  
Hero product reference. Sleek metallic tube in warm rose-gold finish with signature MJ engraving. Creamy, high-shine gloss in a fresh pink-rose shade that catches light beautifully. Maintain exact tube design, color, logo placement, and glossy bullet texture. Never redesign.

**@Image 2 — LIP CLOSE-UP & APPLICATION**  
Reference for extreme beauty macros: perfectly applied glossy lips, micro texture of the gloss, light refraction on the lips, subtle lip movement when speaking or smiling faintly.

**LOCATION**  
Busy Manhattan intersection. Warm late-afternoon golden sunlight. Historic brownstone buildings. Yellow taxis. Crosswalk. Light traffic. Editorial New York energy. Same architecture and atmosphere as the bag film.

**FORMAT**  
Single continuous luxury fashion film. 45 seconds. Horizontal 16:9. Museum-quality realism. No AI look. No CGI. Photoreal filmic beauty.

**DIRECTOR'S NOTES**  
This is not just a lipstick ad — it’s about radiant confidence that never fades. She moves through the fast city on her own terms. The gloss stays impossibly luminous and glossy under direct sunlight, even as she walks, speaks, and smiles. The film celebrates the product’s staying power and the effortless glow it gives her.  
Alternate constantly between:  
• Movement & confidence  
• Beauty close-ups  
• Product texture & application  
• Sunlit glamour  
Every 3–4 seconds the lipstick (or her lips) returns as the hero. Every cut feels expensive. Think Marc Jacobs beauty campaign meets Vogue beauty film.

**COLOR PALETTE**  
Warm golden sunlight, soft babypink tones, rose-gold accents, rich skin tones, honey highlights. Kodak Vision3 film look. Subtle halation. Soft film grain. Natural skin. No teal-orange.

**CAMERA**  
Organic handheld + editorial dolly. Short slider moves. Gentle whip pans. Macro beauty photography. Natural lens breathing. Occasional foreground wipes with taxis/pedestrians.

**LENSES**  
100mm Macro | 85mm Portrait | 50mm | 35mm Street | 24mm Tracking. All behave like real cinema glass with beautiful falloff.

---

**SEGMENT 1 (0.0–6s)**  
Ground level 24mm tracking shot. Camera starts at knee/hip height. Flowing babypink dress hem, strappy sandals, and her confident stride fill the frame. A yellow taxi whips across foreground. At 3 seconds — cut to 100mm macro: she pulls the MARC JACOBS lipstick from her bag, twists it up. Golden sunlight hits the rose-gold tube and the glossy bullet. Extreme texture detail. She applies it smoothly in one fluid motion (mirrorless, on the move). Lips transform into high-shine perfection.

**SEGMENT 2 (6–14s)**  
50mm medium portrait. Camera tracks backward as she walks calmly across the crosswalk. Babypink dress flows gently in the breeze. Hair moves naturally. Effortless, confident expression (soft, knowing half-smile). Golden sunlight kisses her face. Her lips remain mirror-glossy, catching every ray of light. A taxi blurs behind her.  

**CUT to fast 0.3-second macro montage** (lips + product):  
- Gloss catching sunlight, no fading  
- Creamy texture close-up  
- Lip movement as she gently presses lips together  
- Light refracting on the high-shine surface  

**SEGMENT 3 (14–23s)**  
35mm three-quarter tracking shot, matching her walking speed. Side angle. Dress sleeve billows. She turns her head slightly toward the sun, lips prominently featured. Foreground pedestrians and a passing bicycle create natural wipes.  

**CUT to 100mm macro:** Fingers holding the lipstick tube. She twists it down slowly. Rose-gold metal reflects warm sunlight. Subtle embossed logo visible. Natural reflections only.

**SEGMENT 4 (23–32s)**  
85mm hero portrait. She pauses at the corner, golden sunlight wrapping her face and shoulders. Babypink dress looks ethereal. She turns slightly toward the city horizon, then glances toward camera with quiet confidence. Lips still impossibly glossy and fresh. Slow push-in on her face.  

**Voice-over begins** (warm, intimate, luxurious female voice):  
“In the heat of the city… one gloss refuses to fade.”

**SEGMENT 5 (32–40s)**  
Dynamic montage:  
- Walking again (medium shot)  
- Quick macro of lips in direct sun — gloss still perfect  
- She laughs softly (natural micro-expression) — gloss stays vibrant  
- Hand bringing lipstick tube back into frame for a final beauty shot  
- Final macro of lips + tube side-by-side, both glowing in golden hour light  

**Voice-over continues:**  
“Marc Jacobs Summer Gloss. Stays luminous. Stays you.”

**SEGMENT 6 (40–45s)**  
Hero product lock. 100mm macro. Lipstick tube fills frame with perfect sunlight raking across the rose-gold metal and glossy bullet. Hold on the texture. Soft fade to black with elegant MJ logo and product name.

**PERFORMANCE**  
Luxury editorial restraint. Natural walking rhythm. Relaxed shoulders. Real micro-expressions. Confident but never over-posed. Slow breathing. The gloss is the star — always pristine.

**PRODUCT LOCKS**  
Lipstick tube and color must be identical to reference. Extreme gloss retention in sunlight is the hero claim. No color fade. No smudging. Natural lip movement with perfect shine maintained at all times.

**PHYSICS & LIGHTING**  
Dress flows realistically in city wind. Hair reacts naturally. Golden hour sunlight with soft building bounce. Natural skin tones and highlight roll-off. Lip gloss has visible, believable shine and light refraction.

**AUDIO**  
Premium luxury electronic track with warm analog bass and subtle percussion. Layered diegetic city sounds (taxis, footsteps, soft wind). Delicate gloss application sound, fabric rustle, and lipstick cap clicks during macros. Voice-over is intimate and aspirational.
```

A quiet coastal train crossing at sunrise. A stylish young Korean woman @image spends the first hour of her day documenting an unexpected solo adventure after getting off at the wrong station. The atmosphere feels peaceful, adventurous, and emotionally uplifting. Every moment is driven by genuine curiosity instead of performance. The camera language is organic and immersive, constantly discovering new details through fluid movement, natural pauses, spontaneous reactions, environmental interactions, and beautiful landscape composition. Rich cinematic realism, authentic body language, premium natural lighting, subtle wind movement, realistic audio atmosphere, seamless continuity.

Character

 Korean woman in her early twenties. Long naturally flowing dark hair, soft elegant facial features, radiant healthy skin, expressive eyes, relaxed smile, realistic proportions. Wearing a cream knit sweater, loose blue denim jeans, white sneakers, lightweight beige shoulder bag, minimal jewelry.

Storyboard

Unexpected Morning Adventure

Location:
A peaceful seaside railway station surrounded by cliffs, wild flowers, quiet cafés, old railway signs, narrow walking paths, fishing boats, and endless ocean views under warm sunrise light.

(Cut 1 · ~2 sec)
She steps off a nearly empty train, looks around with surprised eyes, laughs softly after realizing this is not her intended stop.

(Cut 2 · ~2 sec)
Walking along a narrow stone path, she notices dozens of colorful windmills spinning beside the sea and reaches out to touch one while smiling.

(Cut 3 · ~2 sec)
She discovers a tiny family-owned bakery, watches fresh bread coming out of the oven through the window, then happily chooses a warm pastry.

(Cut 4 · ~1.5 sec)
Extreme close-up of steam rising from the fresh pastry as she gently tears it apart. Tiny crumbs fall naturally while morning sunlight glows across the frame.

(Cut 5 · ~2 sec)
She sits on a weathered wooden pier feeding curious seagulls before one suddenly lands beside her, making her burst into genuine laughter.

(Cut 6 · ~2 sec)
Following the sound of music, she finds an elderly street violinist performing alone. She quietly listens, closes her eyes for a moment, then applauds with admiration.

(Cut 7 · ~2 sec)
She reaches a cliff viewpoint overlooking the endless coastline. A strong ocean breeze lifts her hair and clothes as she slowly turns toward the breathtaking horizon.

(Cut 8 · ~3 sec)
A vintage train arrives in the distance. She smiles warmly, gives one final wave toward the camera, boards the train, and disappears through the carriage window as the train slowly travels along the coastline beneath the golden morning sky.

Ultra-realistic cinematic filmmaking, emotionally engaging visual storytelling, natural environmental interactions, elegant camera movement, premium travel-film aesthetic, authentic facial expressions, realistic physics, immersive atmosphere, 8K photorealism, visually captivating from beginning to end.

Suddenly, a playful bottlenose dolphin bursts into the frame at high speed, spinning joyfully around the whale before gently tapping its enormous fin as if greeting an old friend.

The whale responds with a deep, melodic whale song and slowly rolls onto its side, inviting the dolphin to swim beside it. Together they glide through the ocean like lifelong companions. The dolphin performs playful flips and spirals around the whale while the whale creates a massive trail of shimmering bubbles that sparkle in the sunlight. A family of sea turtles, manta rays, and schools of glowing fish join them, transforming the scene into a magical underwater celebration.

The camera begins with a sweeping cinematic wide shot, then smoothly tracks alongside the two friends before circling around them in slow motion. It dives beneath them, capturing the whale's enormous silhouette above and the dolphin weaving effortlessly below. The camera finally rises toward the surface as both friends leap out of the ocean at the exact same moment in a spectacular synchronized jump. Water explodes into the air, illuminated by the golden sunset, creating a breathtaking slow-motion finale.

As they splash back into the sea, the camera pulls back to reveal the vast, beautiful ocean, reminding viewers that friendship knows no boundaries even between the largest and one of the smartest creatures in the sea.

Her enemy: a colossal mechanical monster rampaging through the district — a multi-story dark-grey armored titan with magenta warning lights at its joints, smashing a cake-shaped tower into flying cream-colored chunks with one claw.

[0-3s] Combat from the very first second: she opens the film already jet-sliding down a neon street on her thruster sneakers (pink light-flame jetting from the soles, drifting low like a skateboard), candy blaster up and firing — three rainbow energy bolts arc out and burst against the titan's arm in candy-colored star-shaped explosions, particles scattering like sugar sprinkles. The titan roars and swings its claw down — she jet-dashes sideways as the claw pulverizes a lollipop streetlight behind her, candy shards spinning. Camera opens on a low-angle lateral tracking of her slide, punches into a muzzle close-up at the shots, then whips wide as the claw slams down.

[3-6s] High-speed skirmish: she weaves through collapsing candy buildings — riding a toppled chocolate-slab wall diagonally upward, leaping through the ring of the donut roundabout, flipping mid-air while her blaster never stops, bolts raining on the titan's joints. Its shoulder cannons answer, magenta lasers raking the street; she flickers left and right on jet-steps, explosions stitching the ground behind her. The teal mech-cat ejects from her backpack, orbiting her as a floating turret, firing teal micro-bolts in support. High-speed parkour tracking on her route, intercut with nimble close shots of the circling cat.

[6-9s] Melee assault: reading a gap in the titan's attacks, she holsters the blaster and draws the lollipop energy sword — the blade spins open, candy-swirl patterns igniting a pink-yellow energy edge. Jet-dashing in close, she carves upward along the titan's leg armor, every slash bursting plates open in sprays of colored sugar-particle VFX; the titan sweeps its arm — she drops into a sword-braced slide under the limb and answers with a rising reverse cut that shears off a chest plate — exposing the spinning magenta energy core inside. Enraged, the titan opens every gun port in a massive barrage: energy blasts engulf the district, the whole candy city shrouded in colored particles and shockwaves; she's flung airborne, stabilizing with sole-thrusters. Tight tracking through the slash chain, one clear close-up on the exposed core, then a wide pull-back on the city drowned in rainbow blast-light.

[9-12s] Duo combo: through the haze she and the mech-cat exchange a glance — a targeting reticle lights in its eyes. The combo erupts: the cat dives first as a teal bolt of light, plunging into the chest breach and chaining internal explosions that strip the core's armor layer by layer; meanwhile she double-jumps off a hurled chunk of candy-building debris into the sky, sword and blaster both drawn — a rainbow orb charging at the muzzle, the blade's pink-yellow flame at full flare. Time slows: her silhouette hangs against the pink sky, twin weapons blazing. Camera spirals around her airborne charge-up pose.

[12-15s] The finisher: she dives — gun and blade as one: a fully charged rainbow bolt punches the core open, and she follows through, driving the lollipop sword dead-center through it! A colossal candy-colored detonation blooms from within the titan — not fire, but a firework burst of rainbow particles and candy fragments — the machine dissolving and collapsing in the glow. Colored particles drift down like sugar rain, and the ruined streets relight in the radiance — neon reviving block by block, the candy buildings' colors turning vivid again. She lands lightly at the street's center, the mech-cat hopping onto her shoulder; she stows sword and gun, turns to camera with a confident grin, raises both hands in a victory V-sign, and winks. The whole city glitters anew behind her. Freeze frame on this sweet-and-fierce moment, candy particles falling like colored snow.

Style: hyper-saturated futuristic candy world × Japanese anime action × 3D game-CG. Character design strictly consistent throughout: mint-green hair with pink-yellow buns, yellow headphones, CANDY-X jacket, asymmetric mech greave, chunky sneakers, candy blaster and lollipop sword, teal mech-cat. Signature VFX language: all energy effects candy-fied — star-shaped bursts, rainbow bolts, sugar-sprinkle sprays, candy-rain particles — bright and never grim; the titan's magenta is the film's only "villain color" against her pink-teal-yellow. Combat fast and fluid: jet-slides, parkour vaults, gun-to-blade switches, duo combos, all in exhilarating anime-action logic. Palette: pink, teal, yellow, white (girl and city) + magenta (titan). Lighting: bright high-saturation city light, self-glowing energy weapons, final citywide warm relight. Camera: low-angle slide tracking, high-speed parkour chase, close-quarters slash tracking, spiral charge-up orbit, diving finisher, victory freeze — fierce seamless transitions, no hard cuts. 4K, HDR, vivid translucent color.

35mm anamorphic lens, gentle handheld weight, realistic motion blur, smooth fluid 24fps cinematic motion, consistent natural frame rate, no stutter, no dropped frames, no chaotic camera shake, no strobing.

Sickly green and teal palette, Kodak Vision3 500T film emulation, subtle 35mm grain, halation, and anamorphic lens flares. Inspired by tense 1980s jungle laboratory creature thrillers.

AUDIO:

VISUAL REQUIREMENTS:

Realistic creature physics, smooth continuous action, authentic lighting interactions, cinematic depth of field, and seamless motion. ```
CAMERA / LOOK: 由拍摄对象本人使用手持迷你DV摄像机拍摄的镜头。轻微的手抖，偶尔自动对焦搜索，不完美的取景，自然的变焦调整，柔和的胶带般图像质量，细微的颗粒感，真实的自动曝光从柔和的清晨光线中变化。自然的肤色，轻微的运动模糊，真实的消费级摄像机美学。STYLE: 温馨的晨间例行Vlog，带有柔和的ASMR元素。轻松的节奏，最少的对话，真诚的瞬间。焦点放在令人满足的声音上：垫子展开、水瓶盖拧开、轻柔的脚步声、织物拉伸、温柔的呼气。SUBJECT: 二十多岁的年轻东亚女性，【打扮】、高马尾辫，最少饰品。在开始一天之前，平静、专注的能量。SETTING: 为晨间伸展清空的客厅小角落，原木地板。窗帘透入柔和的晨光，一个粉米黄色瑜伽垫，附近一个小植物，安静的氛围。STORYBOARD:→ (2s, propped medium shot) 将摄像机放在架子上，展开垫子。“一天开始前的晨间伸展。”→ (2s, overhead shot) 将垫子平整地抚平在地板上。只有展开的声音。→ (2s, close-up) 坐下，缓慢呼吸，眼睛短暂闭合。→ (2s, handheld shot) 进入一个简单的伸展，调整姿势。“只是慢慢进入状态。”→ (2s, detail shot) 在伸展间隙啜饮水。没有对话。→ (2s, medium shot) 缓慢地进行温柔的流动动作。“动一动感觉真好。”→ (2s, macro shot) 在姿势中赤脚压入垫子。→ (2s, propped shot) 耸肩，深吸一口气。→ (2s, warm ending shot) 平静地坐下，放松的微笑。“现在准备好开始一天了。”→ (1.5s, final shot) 伸手向摄像机。“待会儿见。”手覆盖镜头，录制结束。AUDIO NOTES: 自然的氛围声如垫子展开、水瓶、轻柔呼吸应清晰可闻。对话安静而随意。REALISM NOTES: 真实的肢体语言，自然的眨眼，真诚的平静表情，偶尔停顿呼吸，不完美的取景，对焦呼吸，柔和的晨光变化。应类似于使用消费级摄像机拍摄的真实个人健康Vlog，而非商业制作。
```

Character

JIWON, a Korean female idol in her twenties. Long black hair in a loose ponytail, natural makeup, warm smile, expressive eyes, casual oversized denim jacket over a simple T-shirt, relaxed jeans, sneakers, and a baseball cap.

Location

A cozy modern café during the golden evening. Warm wooden interiors, large windows, hanging pendant lights, fresh pastries displayed in glass cases, espresso machines steaming in the background, indoor plants, handwritten menu boards, soft jazz music, customers chatting naturally, and friendly baristas preparing drinks.

The recording begins just outside the café entrance.

JIWON smiles at the camera while standing in front of the café.

“I got invited to check out this café today, so let’s see what makes it so special.”

She pushes open the glass door and walks inside.

The warm atmosphere immediately surrounds her.

She slowly pans the camera across the interior, showing the cozy seating, pastries, coffee bar, and customers enjoying their drinks.

“It’s even prettier than I expected.”

A friendly barista welcomes her and begins preparing the café’s signature drink.

Holding the camera in one hand, she leans closer to capture the espresso extraction, silky milk steaming, and beautiful latte art.

“Watching this is honestly so satisfying.”

The drink is handed to her.

She smiles, thanks the barista, and takes her first sip.

Her expression immediately brightens.

“Okay… I actually understand why everyone talks about this place.”

She walks toward a window seat, setting the drink on the table while showing a dessert beautifully plated beside it.

She carefully breaks off a piece with a fork.

“This might disappear before I can finish filming.”

She laughs naturally.

Continuing around the café, she notices shelves filled with coffee beans, mugs, and small handmade decorations.

She casually picks up one of the coffee bags.

“I kind of want to take this home.”

She chats briefly with the barista about their best-selling drinks while naturally filming parts of the café, smiling customers, and the relaxing atmosphere.

The camera captures the warm lighting reflecting through the windows as evening slowly settles outside.

She finishes her drink and stands near the entrance.

Turning the camera back toward herself with the café glowing warmly behind her, she smiles.

“Thanks for inviting me. If you’re looking for a cozy place to relax, I definitely recommend stopping by here.”

She gives a small wave.

“I’ll see you next time.”

She pushes open the café door and steps outside into the cool evening air.

### 请严格参考这张九宫格作为镜头调度依据，但不要逐格硬切复现，不要做九格轮播，而是把九宫格中的空间、人物、群像、细节关系，转化为一段连续流动、极具时代意味的15秒纪…

#### 📝 Prompt

```
请严格参考这张九宫格作为镜头调度依据，但不要逐格硬切复现，不要做九格轮播，而是把九宫格中的空间、人物、群像、细节关系，转化为一段连续流动、极具时代意味的15秒纪录片式电影镜头。整体必须像中国旧港口劳动现场的真实历史影像，像被偶然保留下来的珍贵纪录片片段。上世纪中国老码头氛围，灰白天光，轻雾与潮湿水汽，江边旧船、吊臂、缆绳、驳岸、沿岸老建筑，蓝灰工装、白色搪瓷茶缸、香烟、旧自行车，去饱和蓝灰与铁锈褐色调，重颗粒16mm修复胶片质感，真实、粗粝、克制、厚重，细节锐利。

0-3秒：参考左中格和中下格，先用较宽的环境中景建立旧码头空间。摄影机站在码头边缘，轻微手持，缓慢向前推进。画面里有江边空地、旧船、吊臂、来往工人、旧自行车、岸边建筑和灰白天空，空间层次清晰，空气中有潮湿薄雾和旧工业气息。前景允许有人影、肩背、自行车车把自然掠过镜头，形成柔和遮挡，让镜头像真实进入现场，而不是摆拍开场。整体是纪录片式观察视角，先交代时代与地点。

3-7秒：参考左上格和中中格，镜头顺势进入工人群体内部。几位穿蓝灰工装的中年码头工人围坐在江边石阶、木桩或岸边休息聊天，有人蹲着，有人坐着，有人半倚着，动作自然松弛，像刚结束劳作后的短暂歇息。有人低声说话，有人露出克制笑意，有人捧着白色搪瓷茶缸喝水。摄影机不要切断，而是在他们之间缓慢横移，保持轻微呼吸感，像摄影记者在群体中寻找真正值得停留的人物。人物不看镜头，不要表演痕迹，不要舞台感。

7-11秒：参考上中格和左下格，把视觉重心自然吸附到那位吸烟工人身上。镜头从群像中缓慢靠近一位中年码头工人的侧脸中近景，他皮肤粗粝，脸上有风吹日晒的纹理，穿旧蓝色工装，嘴里叼着烟，手指夹烟的动作非常生活化，眼神越过同伴望向江面或远处船只。这里不是戏剧化痛苦，而是一种劳动间隙短暂放空的沉静。镜头停留片刻，让烟雾、手指、脸部纹理、旧布料、背景虚化中的吊臂和船身形成最有重量的一瞬。随后参考右上格，让另一个工人举起白色搪瓷茶缸进入前景，茶缸或肩背自然遮挡镜头，完成一次无痕转场，把人物从个人沉思重新带回群体关系。

11-15秒：参考右中格、右下格和中下格，镜头轻轻后撤并微微抬起，把几位工人重新放回整个码头环境之中。群像重新完整：有人笑着接话，有人端着茶缸，有人侧身倾听，吸烟工人的神情也略微松开，回到同伴之间。远处旧船、吊臂、缆绳、江边地面、散落器具、来往工人和沿岸建筑共同构成厚重时代空间。结尾不要定格式摆拍，不要口号式升华，不要戏剧性煽情，只停留在工人仍在说笑、茶缸仍在手中、烟雾在风里飘散、码头生活仍在继续的状态里，像镜头只是偶然停下，时代本身并没有结束。

镜头语言要求：全程连续流动，不硬切，不拼贴，不逐格复现九宫格。必须使用纪录片电影语言，轻微手持但稳定，缓慢推进、轻微横移、顺势绕行、前景遮挡转场、柔和景深变化。构图重点是“环境中的人物”“群体中的个体”“个体重新回到群体”，形成完整叙事流动。不要广告感，不要MV感，不要现代服装，不要现代城市元素，不要鲜艳颜色，不要刻意煽情。整体气质必须安静、粗粝、真实、克制，像国家影像档案中一段珍贵的中国旧港口生活纪录片。
```

### Real live Tour de France motorcycle camera broadcast, Stage 14 mountain climb…

#### 📝 Prompt

```
Real live Tour de France motorcycle camera broadcast, Stage 14 mountain climb. Continuous single-take tracking shot filmed from a race moto running parallel then slightly behind the rider. Harsh high-altitude daylight, sweat, dust, realistic road vibration, authentic crowd energy, subtle broadcast grain and compression.

Use the provided still as the strict visual reference for TechHalla’s exact face, bald head, full dark beard, yellow RaboBank leader jersey, body proportions, bike and mountain-road environment. Do not alter his appearance.

0-4s: [Continuous side tracking] Camera locked parallel to TechHalla as he climbs out of the saddle with extreme effort, heavy pedaling, face clenched, sweat flying. Dense fans on both sides waving Spanish and Basque flags, shouting, running along the barriers.

4-7s: [Tracking continues, slight pull-back] A spontaneous elderly woman wearing a floral bata bursts from the left roadside and sprints at full speed, catching up until she is running right next to the still-pedaling TechHalla.

7-12s: [Tight parallel tracking] The elderly woman suddenly jumps with both hands and latches onto TechHalla’s back and jersey from behind, clinging tightly while the cyclist keeps pedaling hard up the steep gradient. TechHalla’s body reacts to the sudden extra weight — deeper lean, heavier breathing, face more strained — but he never stops pedaling. The elderly woman holds on as they move forward together. Crowd goes completely wild.

12-15s: [Camera shifts smoothly behind] TechHalla continues climbing with the elderly woman still hanging on his back, then powers harder on the pedals, pulling slightly ahead as the camera follows from the rear along the open mountain road.

Photorealistic 8K broadcast footage, perfect motion blur on wheels and running legs, realistic physics of the jump, body impact and weight transfer, stable character face and jersey, natural sweat and fabric stretch under the grab, coherent crowd motion, cinematic motorcycle-camera shake, no artifacts, movie-level temporal consistency.
```

### 生成15秒、9:16竖版、高端古装女频短剧第3集，延续前两集剧情。参考@女主近景肖像和女主三视图，保持女主同一张脸、同一发型、同一服装、富家千金白月光气质；参考…

#### 📝 Prompt

```
生成15秒、9:16竖版、高端古装女频短剧第3集，延续前两集剧情。参考@女主近景肖像和女主三视图，保持女主同一张脸、同一发型、同一服装、富家千金白月光气质；参考@男主近景肖像和男主三视图，保持男主同一张脸、同一发型、同一服装、俊美贵公子气质。表小姐@表小姐近景肖像和男主三视图：年轻成年东方女性，外表温柔端庄、内里心机刻薄；新增角色贴身丫鬟：年轻成年东方女性，神色怯弱、明显害怕。不要画面文字，台词自然口型说出。真实古装短剧质感，不要插画、二次元、数字人CG感。

场景：雨后清晨的古风回廊与庭院交界处，青石地面微湿，廊柱整齐，远处花木带水气，空气清冷。自然晨光带一点冷白雨后光感，人物面部有柔和补光，整体清透细腻，带轻微紧张感。背景音乐前段为低缓古风弦乐，后段转为压迫感更强的低音古风配乐。

0-3秒：表小姐短暂慌乱后迅速稳住神色，转身指向身后的贴身丫鬟，眼神凌厉，表面委屈，语气却急促发狠：“定是这贱婢私自妄为，与我无关。”贴身丫鬟脸色发白，立刻跪下，肩膀发抖，低着头慌忙认罪：“是奴婢一人所为。”镜头先给表小姐近景，再切丫鬟跪地特写，突出她惊恐不安。环境有轻微风声，音乐压低。

3-6秒：表小姐立刻接话，想快速收场，神情恢复几分镇定，冷声下令：“拖下去，杖责二十。”两名家丁上前拉人。丫鬟明显害怕，身体发软，却不敢求饶，只偷偷抬眼看向表小姐，眼底带求救和恐惧。女主一直站在一旁冷静观察，眼神越来越冷，察觉不对。镜头切双人中景，再给女主近景，捕捉她从沉静到生疑的变化。

6-8秒：女主忽然开口，声音不高，却极有压迫感：“慢着。”语气冷静、克制、不容违抗。全场动作一顿，家丁停手，表小姐表情微僵，男主侧目看向女主，眼神沉静而赞许。镜头快速推近女主脸部，突出她清醒掌控局势的气场。音乐在“慢着”之后停顿半拍。

8-11秒：男主向前一步，神色冷静锋利，从袖中缓缓拿出一枚断裂的院落令牌，放到众人眼前，语气平稳却带压迫感：“昨夜能调走护卫的，是表小姐院中的令牌。”镜头先给令牌特写，再切男主近景。表小姐脸上的镇定瞬间裂开，呼吸一滞，瞳孔微缩。丫鬟也怔住，脸色更白。音乐加一声低沉转折音效，气氛骤然收紧。

11-13秒：表小姐强撑镇定，嘴唇微动，想辩解却一时失语。女主缓步走近她，目光锋利，神情平静却极具压迫感，语气轻缓却字字诛心：“你这么急着罚她，是怕她认罪，还是怕她开口？”镜头切女主和表小姐对峙中近景，再给表小姐面部特写，表现她强撑镇定后的明显慌乱和心虚后退半步。

13-15秒：被押住的贴身丫鬟情绪崩溃，忽然抬头哭喊，声音发颤、带绝望：“奴婢只奉命拔钉，藏在屋里的信……不是奴婢放的！”女主和男主同时神色一变，眼神瞬间凝重，意识到背后还有更深的黑手。表小姐脸色惨白，彻底慌了。镜头最后定格在丫鬟崩溃喊出真相、女主男主同时变色的瞬间，音乐以短促而悬疑的古风收束音结束。

要求：不要字幕，不要文字；台词口型自然；女主前半段冷静观察，后半段强势控场；男主沉稳护场、拿出关键证据；表小姐从急于脱身到强撑镇定再到慌乱失控；丫鬟从害怕顶罪到崩溃说漏真正线索。保持人物脸部一致性、古装真实质感、镜头流畅、节奏紧凑，有冲突、有爽点、有新钩子。
```

### FORMAT: 15s / 9 shots / 16:9 / 1280x720 / 24fps,

#### 📝 Prompt

```
FORMAT: 15s / 9 shots / 16:9 / 1280x720 / 24fps,
high-detail cinematic anime adventure commercial,
smooth multi-shot narrative, dynamic aerial cinematography,
stable camera movement and luminous natural color grade.
[STYLE]
Premium hand-painted anime commercial aesthetic with clean cel shading,
soft painterly environmental lighting, dramatic Himalayan landscapes,
volumetric white clouds, bright blue skies, warm golden sunlight,
subtle atmospheric haze, expressive character animation,
dynamic perspective, cinematic depth and gentle film grain.
The final result should look like a professionally animated adventure
commercial rather than live-action footage or low-quality CGI.
[SCENE]
A professional bungee-jumping platform suspended high above a vast
mountain valley near Pokhara, Nepal.
The environment remains consistent throughout all shots:
forest-covered Himalayan mountains, a winding river far below,
bright daylight, drifting clouds, distant mountain haze,
natural wind and a deep sense of height and scale.
The jump takes place under controlled professional conditions.
[CHARACTER]
One young adult male adventure traveler with short dark hair,
an athletic build, medium skin tone and reflective orange-red
sports sunglasses.
He wears:
a teal short-sleeve T-shirt,
khaki cargo shorts,
black athletic shoes,
black wristbands,
secure ankle cuffs,
and a professional black backup safety harness.
His face, hairstyle, sunglasses, clothing, body proportions,
harness, footwear and accessories remain exactly consistent
throughout the entire video.
Before jumping, he appears focused and slightly nervous.
During the fall, his expression gradually changes into
excitement, freedom and joy.
[REFERENCES]
@image1 = primary hero reference for the jumper’s appearance,
wardrobe, equipment, platform, cord colors and mountain location.
@Image2 = storyboard reference for shot order, composition,
anime visual style, camera angles and narrative progression.
Follow the storyboard sequence from Scene 01 to Scene 09.
Do not reproduce the storyboard sheet, labels, panels or written text
inside the actual video.
[SHOTS]
[00:00–00:01]
Shot 1 — THE EDGE:
MEDIUM CLOSE-UP + slow push-in —
steadies —
the jumper stands at the very edge of the metal platform,
takes a controlled breath and looks down toward the immense valley.
His hands briefly tighten around the harness straps.
A light mountain wind moves his shirt.
The platform and safety equipment are clearly visible.
[00:01–00:02]
Shot 2 — TAKING THE PLUNGE:
WIDE SIDE SHOT + gentle lateral tracking —
launches —
the jumper confidently pushes away from the platform
with both arms extended forward.
His body leaves the platform cleanly and naturally.
The secured bungee cord follows behind without tangling.
[00:02–00:04]
Shot 3 — FREE FALL:
WIDE SIDE PROFILE + descending chase movement —
falls —
the camera follows beside the jumper during rapid free fall.
His body remains horizontal with arms extended.
The forest-covered mountains rush past in the background
with controlled directional motion blur.
[00:04–00:06]
Shot 4 — THE STRETCH:
EXTREME WIDE SHOT + crane downward —
stretches —
the bungee cord gradually reaches full tension above the valley.
The jumper’s descent slows naturally.
His body forms a smooth downward arc as the elastic cord stretches.
Show the immense scale of the valley, river and mountains below.
[00:06–00:08]
Shot 5 — THE BOUNCE:
LOW-ANGLE WIDE SHOT + upward tilt —
rebounds —
the stretched cord safely pulls the jumper upward.
His body rises naturally through the air.
The bungee rope remains properly aligned and securely connected.
Sunlight shines around his silhouette as he moves back toward the sky.
[00:08–00:10]
Shot 6 — JOY IN THE AIR:
FRONT MEDIUM CLOSE-UP + smooth orbit —
celebrates —
the jumper opens both arms, laughs and releases an excited yell.
His reflective sunglasses catch the sunlight.
Wind moves his shirt and hair naturally.
His expression communicates adrenaline, relief and pure joy.
[00:10–00:12]
Shot 7 — VIEW OF FREEDOM:
EPIC EXTREME WIDE SHOT + slow panoramic pull-back —
swings —
the jumper swings freely above the valley while looking toward
the mountain horizon.
Reveal the winding river, green forests, distant peaks,
bright clouds and warm sunlight.
Create a powerful feeling of freedom, scale and adventure.
[00:12–00:14]
Shot 8 — ONE MORE TIME:
WIDE SIDE TRACKING SHOT + smooth parallel movement —
glides —
the jumper completes one final controlled swing across the valley.
His arms remain extended like wings.
The camera travels beside him while the forest background
passes with clean cinematic motion.
[00:14–00:15]
Shot 9 — LIVE BOLDLY:
WIDE HERO SHOT + slow pull-back —
embraces —
the jumper faces the glowing mountain horizon with both arms open.
Warm sunlight breaks through the clouds behind him.
The bungee cord and safety harness remain clearly visible.
End on an uplifting cinematic silhouette with clean empty space
on the right side of the frame for a slogan to be added later.
Do not generate text inside the video.
[AUDIO / SFX]
Create synchronized cinematic audio.
00:00–00:01:
Soft mountain wind, distant birds, subtle heartbeat,
quiet metal platform creak and controlled breathing.
00:01–00:02:
Strong launch whoosh, harness strap movement
and a short musical rise.
00:02–00:04:
Rapidly intensifying wind rush and deep cinematic bass build.
00:04–00:06:
Elastic cord stretching sound,
low-frequency tension effect and increasing heartbeat.
00:06–00:08:
Powerful but controlled elastic rebound,
deep “boing” impact and upward wind whoosh.
00:08–00:10:
Natural excited yell, joyful laughter and rushing wind.
JUMPER:
“Woohoo!”
00:10–00:14:
Uplifting orchestral adventure music,
natural wind, distant birds and subtle valley ambience.
00:14–00:15:
Music reaches an inspiring cinematic hit,
then resolves into soft mountain wind.
[CONTINUITY]
Maintain exactly the same character identity across all nine shots.
Maintain the same:
face,
hairstyle,
sunglasses,
teal shirt,
khaki shorts,
black shoes,
wristbands,
ankle attachments,
safety harness,
platform design,
bungee cord colors,
weather,
sun direction,
mountain geography
and anime rendering style.
The bungee cord must remain securely connected to the professional
ankle cuffs and backup harness throughout the entire jump.
Use believable gravity, body weight, cord tension, acceleration,
deceleration, rebound movement, fabric motion and hair movement.
Transitions between shots must be clean and intentional.
Each shot should flow naturally into the following shot.
[CAMERA RULES]
Use only one controlled camera movement per shot.
Smooth cinematic movement.
Stable horizon.
Professional framing.
Natural lens perspective.
Controlled motion blur.
Sharp subject clarity.
No random zooms.
No uncontrolled camera shaking.
No abrupt direction changes.
[CONSTRAINTS]
Professional supervised bungee jump only.
Realistic safety equipment.
Natural body movement.
Correct human anatomy.
Stable facial identity.
Consistent anime character design.
Sharp image quality.
Natural colors.
Cinematic texture.
High-detail landscape.
No flickering between scenes.
Do not show the storyboard layout inside the video.
Do not generate panel borders, scene numbers, subtitles,
captions, watermarks or interface elements.
Reserve clean negative space in the final shot so that
“LIVE BOLDLY.” can be added during editing.
```

### [STYLE + CAMERA + ATMOSPHERE]

![English](https://img.shields.io/badge/lang-English-blue)

#### 📝 Prompt

```
[STYLE + CAMERA + ATMOSPHERE]
Real live Tour de France motorcycle camera broadcast, Stage 14 mountain climb. Continuous single-take tracking shot filmed from a race moto running parallel then slightly behind the rider. Harsh high-altitude daylight, sweat, dust, realistic road vibration, authentic crowd energy, subtle broadcast grain and compression.
[IMAGE REFERENCES]
Use the provided still of the man riding the ostrich + the Alex character sheet as the strict visual reference for Alex Patrascu’s exact face (short dark hair with grey, short dense beard/stubble), body proportions, yellow Ostrich Racing leader jersey, white aero helmet, black bib shorts, the ostrich with its saddle and packs, and the mountain-road environment. Do not alter his appearance.
[TIMELINE SECOND BY SECOND]
0-4s: [Continuous side tracking] Camera locked parallel to Alex Patrascu as he rides the ostrich hard up the climb, body crouched and driving the bird forward with extreme effort, face clenched, sweat flying. Dense fans on both sides waving Spanish and Basque flags, shouting, running along the barriers.
4-7s: [Tracking continues, slight pull-back] A spontaneous fan in casual clothes bursts from the left roadside holding a big Spanish bocadillo in one hand and an open beer can in the other. He sprints at full speed, catches up and runs parallel right next to the still-striding ostrich and Alex.
7-12s: [Tight parallel tracking] Without slowing the ostrich, Alex reaches out with his right hand, firmly grabs the bocadillo, takes one massive bite, then clearly extends his arm and hands the bocadillo back to the fan. Immediately after, he grabs the open beer can from the fan’s other hand and takes a huge, desperate gulp while still driving the ostrich hard uphill. Beer spills slightly down his beard. Crowd explodes in cheers.
12-15s: [Camera shifts smoothly behind] Alex throws the can back, rises taller in the saddle and urges the ostrich into a full acceleration. He pulls away up the steep road as the camera follows from the rear, showing the open mountain road ahead.
[STYLE & QUALITY BOOSTERS]
Photorealistic 8K broadcast footage, perfect motion blur on the ostrich’s powerful running legs and the fan’s sprinting legs, realistic physics of running + ostrich-riding interaction, clear hand-off of the bocadillo then the beer can, stable character face and jersey, natural sweat and fabric movement, coherent crowd motion, cinematic motorcycle-camera shake, no artifacts, movie-level temporal consistency.

### 同じプロンプトでも参照画像（白黒絵コンテ／詳細ストーリーボード／フルカラー漫画／配置図など）を変えてSeedanceの出力差を比較し、狙いの動きに合う参照画像形…

![日本語](https://img.shields.io/badge/lang-日本語-green)

#### 📝 Prompt

```
同じプロンプトでも参照画像（白黒絵コンテ／詳細ストーリーボード／フルカラー漫画／配置図など）を変えてSeedanceの出力差を比較し、狙いの動きに合う参照画像形式を選ぶ  
Seedanceでは文字プロンプトよりv2v（Blender v2vなど）の参照動画入力のほうが動きや内容の制御が強く効くため、狙い通りにしたい場合はv2vを優先して使う  
絵コンテ参照はラフでもよく、ラフなほうが動きが出やすいので、まずはラフ絵コンテを参照画像として試す
```

#### 💡 Tips

同じプロンプトでも参照画像（白黒絵コンテ／詳細ストーリーボード／フルカラー漫画／配置図など）を変えてSeedanceの出力差を比較し、狙いの動きに合う参照画像形式を選ぶ  
Seedanceでは文字プロンプトよりv2v（Blender v2vなど）の参照動画入力のほうが動きや内容の制御が強く効くため、狙い通りにしたい場合はv2vを優先して使う  
絵コンテ参照はラフでもよく、ラフなほうが動きが出やすいので、まずはラフ絵コンテを参照画像として試す

### Use a single prompt in Seedance 2 that includes genre/style + quality targets…

![English](https://img.shields.io/badge/lang-English-blue)

#### 📝 Prompt

```
Use a single prompt in Seedance 2 that includes genre/style + quality targets (High-energy 3D CGI animated comedy short, Pixar quality, ultra-detailed, 4K render, 24fps) to steer the overall look and output specs.  
Add explicit character casting blocks (age, build, hair, wardrobe, signature behaviors like mustache twitches) to lock character consistency across shots.  
Structure the prompt into timestamped shots (e.g., SHOT 1 0.00–5.00s, SHOT 2 5.00–10.00s, SHOT 3 10.00–15.00s) with shot size and camera direction (close-up low angle snap push; medium whip-pan into tracking; wide to close-up slow dolly then snap push) to control pacing and camera movement.  
Specify lighting and color transitions per shot (warm pendant light/orange stove-flare glow shifting to cool dusk streetlight) to guide scene mood changes.  
Call out continuity requirements explicitly (perfect continuity of the pizza box and both characters; patch clearly visible) to preserve props and readable details across cuts.  
Include sound design cues in the prompt (upbeat energetic sound design with whooshes and impacts) to influence audio styling if supported.  
End with an explicit finishing instruction (freeze on his expression, held finish) to control the final frame/ending beat.
```

#### 💡 Tips

Use a single prompt in Seedance 2 that includes genre/style + quality targets (High-energy 3D CGI animated comedy short, Pixar quality, ultra-detailed, 4K render, 24fps) to steer the overall look and output specs.  
Add explicit character casting blocks (age, build, hair, wardrobe, signature behaviors like mustache twitches) to lock character consistency across shots.  
Structure the prompt into timestamped shots (e.g., SHOT 1 0.00–5.00s, SHOT 2 5.00–10.00s, SHOT 3 10.00–15.00s) with shot size and camera direction (close-up low angle snap push; medium whip-pan into tracking; wide to close-up slow dolly then snap push) to control pacing and camera movement.  
Specify lighting and color transitions per shot (warm pendant light/orange stove-flare glow shifting to cool dusk streetlight) to guide scene mood changes.  
Call out continuity requirements explicitly (perfect continuity of the pizza box and both characters; patch clearly visible) to preserve props and readable details across cuts.  
Include sound design cues in the prompt (upbeat energetic sound design with whooshes and impacts) to influence audio styling if supported.  
End with an explicit finishing instruction (freeze on his expression, held finish) to control the final frame/ending beat.

### Use Gemini to lock the character sheet before generating fast-motion scenes…

![English](https://img.shields.io/badge/lang-English-blue)

#### 📝 Prompt

```
Use Gemini to lock the character sheet before generating fast-motion scenes, since fast movement exposes inconsistencies.  
Feed past episodes into Claude as context when writing the next script to maintain continuity.  
In Seedance 2.0, use image-to-video to keep the character’s face locked during mid-motion shots.  
For fast-motion sequences where hands/props warp, generate in short bursts instead of long takes.  
Re-roll any Seedance clip where a hand or object doesn’t match the last frame to maintain continuity.
```

#### 💡 Tips

Use Gemini to lock the character sheet before generating fast-motion scenes, since fast movement exposes inconsistencies.  
Feed past episodes into Claude as context when writing the next script to maintain continuity.  
In Seedance 2.0, use image-to-video to keep the character’s face locked during mid-motion shots.  
For fast-motion sequences where hands/props warp, generate in short bursts instead of long takes.  
Re-roll any Seedance clip where a hand or object doesn’t match the last frame to maintain continuity.

### Build a consistent dancer identity first (body type, height, hairstyle, outfit…

![English](https://img.shields.io/badge/lang-English-blue)

#### 📝 Prompt

```
Build a consistent dancer identity first (body type, height, hairstyle, outfit style, facial expression, posture, performance energy) and reuse that same influencer model across videos to maintain continuity in Seedance 2.0 outputs.  
Generate a strong first frame (anchoring character, outfit, pose, camera angle, lighting, location, mood) before running Seedance 2.0 so the video model has a clear visual starting point.  
Create a 16-panel storyboard from the first frame to map beat-by-beat choreography and transitions, then use that storyboard as the movement guide for Seedance 2.0 image-to-video generation.  
Run Seedance 2.0 via an image-to-video workflow using the assets in order: influencer model → first frame → 16-panel storyboard → motion prompt → video, instead of relying on text-only prompting.  
Write the Seedance 2.0 video prompt to reinforce the storyboard (timing, camera behavior, lighting, realism) and avoid introducing new outfits, locations, camera styles, or unrelated movements that conflict with the storyboard.  
Iterate by reviewing motion, refining the storyboard, and regenerating to progressively improve choreography control and continuity.
```

#### 💡 Tips

Build a consistent dancer identity first (body type, height, hairstyle, outfit style, facial expression, posture, performance energy) and reuse that same influencer model across videos to maintain continuity in Seedance 2.0 outputs.  
Generate a strong first frame (anchoring character, outfit, pose, camera angle, lighting, location, mood) before running Seedance 2.0 so the video model has a clear visual starting point.  
Create a 16-panel storyboard from the first frame to map beat-by-beat choreography and transitions, then use that storyboard as the movement guide for Seedance 2.0 image-to-video generation.

Iterate by reviewing motion, refining the storyboard, and regenerating to progressively improve choreography control and continuity.

### Use Seedance 2.0 with Higgsfield by dropping the prompt into Higgsfield and…

![English](https://img.shields.io/badge/lang-English-blue)

#### 📝 Prompt

```
Use Seedance 2.0 with Higgsfield by dropping the prompt into Higgsfield and attaching both character reference sheets as @jungle-prince and @phoenix-drake to lock identity consistency.

Add an ACTIVE REFERENCES section with 100% matches the reference and identical every frame for each tagged subject to prevent design drift across frames.

Specify a non-empty FIRST FRAME / BLOCKING description (subjects already in motion, nothing frozen) to avoid static or empty opening frames.

Force a single-shot result by explicitly prompting Single continuous take, 15s and reiterating no cuts in FORMAT MODE.

Lock camera behavior by stating a chase camera locked BEHIND for the entire clip with a fixed distance (0.5–0.8m) to keep consistent framing.

Control motion blur by specifying 180° shutter motion blur and no drift in the OPTICS/CAMERA sections.

Direct a speed ramp by prompting one in-camera speed ramp into super slow motion at a specific timestamp window (4–6s) and a hard snap back to real-time.

Use a wide-angle look by specifying 107° FOV, wide rectilinear, prime-lens character to shape perspective and lens feel.

Maintain subject framing during focus effects by instructing the camera to keep rear position and rack focus onto a butterfly at the lens during the slow-mo beat.

Prevent unwanted audio/text artifacts by explicitly prompting NO MUSIC, SFX ONLY, no dialogue, no subtitles, no on-screen text, no watermark.

Reinforce key constraints with a POSITIVE LOCKS section (camera stays behind, colossal scale held, exact reference design every frame, eyes stay natural/no eye glow, contact shadows read clearly) to reduce model deviations.
```

### Use Seedance 2.0’s multi-input generation by providing two images + two videos…

![English](https://img.shields.io/badge/lang-English-blue)

#### 📝 Prompt

```
Use Seedance 2.0’s multi-input generation by providing two images + two videos + audio in one run, and instruct in the prompt how to combine them (e.g., which source supplies character vs background vs motion vs sound).

To swap a character/background in a finished video, attach/tag the character and background reference assets in the prompt and explicitly request the swap.

To preserve original camera and character choreography, include an instruction like keep the same movement so Seedance retains the source motion exactly.

For virtual outfit try-on on real footage, use the real person video as the base reference and prompt for outfit replacement while preserving the face to avoid distortion.

To localize an ad while changing the actor, prompt for language translation plus actor swap in the same generation, and instruct that every gesture/movement must be preserved for A/B testing.

To extend a short clip, feed the 3-second video as reference and prompt what happens next, explicitly requesting character consistency across the continuation.

Generate Seedance 2.0 prompts with Claude (Opus 4.6) instead of writing them manually, as it’s reported to handle vision-model prompting better than other LLMs.

Create separate Claude Skills (instruction files) per content type (cinematic, animation, product ad, dialogue) and use the matching skill to generate Seedance prompts rather than using one universal prompt template.

For cinematic scenes, use a Cinematic Claude Skill that forces real camera language (dolly/crane/rack focus) and shot discipline across multiple cuts, and specify duration and shot count (e.g., 15s, three shots).

For animation, use an Animation Claude Skill that locks style and physics at the top of the prompt before shot construction to keep the look consistent.

For product ads, use a Product Ad Claude Skill that first builds a full product description from the reference image, then enforces hero framing/commercial lighting so the product stays centered in every shot.

For dialogue scenes, use a Dialogue Claude Skill that explicitly coordinates lip sync, emotional direction, shot structure, and audio cues in the prompt so timing/pauses/tone land as written.

Have the Claude Skill ask (and you answer) setup questions like genre, tone, shot count, and camera energy before it outputs a fully structured Seedance 2.0 prompt.
```

#### 💡 Tips

Use Seedance 2.0’s multi-input generation by providing two images + two videos + audio in one run, and instruct in the prompt how to combine them (e.g., which source supplies character vs background vs motion vs sound).

To swap a character/background in a finished video, attach/tag the character and background reference assets in the prompt and explicitly request the swap.

To preserve original camera and character choreography, include an instruction like keep the same movement so Seedance retains the source motion exactly.

For virtual outfit try-on on real footage, use the real person video as the base reference and prompt for outfit replacement while preserving the face to avoid distortion.

To localize an ad while changing the actor, prompt for language translation plus actor swap in the same generation, and instruct that every gesture/movement must be preserved for A/B testing.

To extend a short clip, feed the 3-second video as reference and prompt what happens next, explicitly requesting character consistency across the continuation.

Generate Seedance 2.0 prompts with Claude (Opus 4.6) instead of writing them manually, as it’s reported to handle vision-model prompting better than other LLMs.

Create separate Claude Skills (instruction files) per content type (cinematic, animation, product ad, dialogue) and use the matching skill to generate Seedance prompts rather than using one universal prompt template.

For cinematic scenes, use a Cinematic Claude Skill that forces real camera language (dolly/crane/rack focus) and shot discipline across multiple cuts, and specify duration and shot count (e.g., 15s, three shots).

For animation, use an Animation Claude Skill that locks style and physics at the top of the prompt before shot construction to keep the look consistent.

For product ads, use a Product Ad Claude Skill that first builds a full product description from the reference image, then enforces hero framing/commercial lighting so the product stays centered in every shot.

For dialogue scenes, use a Dialogue Claude Skill that explicitly coordinates lip sync, emotional direction, shot structure, and audio cues in the prompt so timing/pauses/tone land as written.

Have the Claude Skill ask (and you answer) setup questions like genre, tone, shot count, and camera energy before it outputs a fully structured Seedance 2.0 prompt.
